## DAT-08

Build validated corpus releases. Build from actually ready sources, join exact processing runs and verify chunk/vector counts and hashes. Publish only complete validated releases; report a partial corpus as partial. DAT-03 blocks inclusion of the unresolved pilot, not releases of other ready or authored sources.

## RET-01

## RET-02

Implement pgvector storage and search. Persist release-scoped vectors and exact cosine search with integrity constraints. Verify expected ranking on a small numeric fixture and reject mixed dimensions or duplicate embeddings.

## GEN-01

Implement separate chat and MCQ schemas. ChatResponseV1 accepts full natural-language answers, compact answers, clarification/social/refusal and cited evidence; MCQResponseV1 preserves the old eight fields in evaluation only. Python/JSON Schema/OpenAPI fixtures agree and chat requires no choices. Generation handover migration: Create discriminated ChatResponseV1/MCQResponseV1 types. Chat has no choices; MCQ alone requires A–D and normalised duplicate checks. Align schemas, Python, OpenAPI and clients.

## GEN-02

Implement model adapters. Deliver deterministic mock and configurable hosted/local live adapters with usage, latency and standard errors. Missing live configuration must not fall back silently; preserve the model_mode marker. Generation handover migration: Adapt to shared messages and request_context; keep synchronous worker execution, add provider errors/request identifiers/usage, and handle empty choices, truncation and configuration failures. Deliver one configured provider; examples do not prove support.

## GEN-03

Version chat and benchmark prompts separately. Interactive prompts consume selected session context, profile and actual evidence. Open-answer benchmarks receive stem-only input; MCQ receives symmetric choices. E0 is evidence-free, E1 is grounded, and no gold or test-only bias enters generation. Generation handover migration: Preserve historical templates; version chat/OpenQA/MCQ prompts without test hooks. Move mock behaviour into test adapters, not live prompts. Supply real history, summary and profile to chat.

## GEN-04

Implement conversational response policy. Distinguish clear factual questions needing evidence from social messages and missing-referent clarification. Empty evidence can refuse a factual request, but must not automatically reject greetings or clarifying turns; nonempty evidence does not prove support. Generation handover migration: Remove oracle status inputs. Empty evidence yields NO_EVIDENCE only for a clear factual query. Handle greetings/clarification separately; nonempty evidence is not sufficiency. Label changes must not alter provider input.

- Current observation: September 13 current scoped components: The same 20-question live suites observed 17/20, 19/20 and 20/20 automated passes through actual HTTP/worker persistence, official v5 OpenStax, real E5/pgvector/MiniLM retrieval and DeepSeek deepseek-flash. Earlier empty response, strict format/citation repair, explicit-topic preparation and evidence-coverage failures remain stored; the citation validator was not relaxed. Attempt 2 review covers 20 responses/90 submitted snapshots/46 citations; attempt 3 covers 20/196/81. Read-only checks bind actual requests, frozen model/profile/policy and citations to exact text hashes, physical locators, processing and 384D release membership. The separate initial smoke review covers one answer/three citations, not all of suite attempt 1.

Both frozen 256-condition prefix baselines are terminal with all outcomes retained and negative dense-E1 diagnostics. Independent scientific/relevance/teaching review and broad semantic coverage remain unverified.
